\displaystyle=\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}\left[\int|K(s)|\,\mathrm{d}s\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}\int[K(s)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\|{G[f]}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\iint\left|{G[f](x,y)}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle=\iint\left|{\iint f(x+sh,y+th)K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\leq\iint\left[\iint\left|{f(x+sh,y+th)K(s)K(t)}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\iint\left[\iint\left|{f(x+sh-th,y)}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint\left[\iint\left|{f(x^{\prime},y)}\right|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}\left[\int\{K(s)\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}.

Finally,

\displaystyle\int\left[\int G[f](x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\iiint f(x+sh,y+th)K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\leq\iint\left\{\int\left[\int f(x+sh,y+th)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right\}\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\iint\left\{\int\left[\int f(x+sh-th,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right\}\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\lesssim\iint\left\{\int\left[\int|f(x+sh-th,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right\}\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\left\{\int\left[\int|f(x^{\prime},y)|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right\}\left\{\iint\left[K(s)K(t)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int\left[\int|f(x^{\prime},y)|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y).

Next, we note that

\displaystyle\left\|{f_{1}}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}

\displaystyle=\iint\frac{1}{h^{d}}\left|{K\left(\frac{x-y}{h}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle=\iint\left|{K(x^{\prime})}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left|{K(x^{\prime})}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime},\text{ and}

\displaystyle\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}

\displaystyle=\iint\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\left|{\int K\left(\frac{x-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-z}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\leq\iint\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(y^{\prime}\right)}\right|\left[\int\eta_{0}(z+hy^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}z\right]\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\left[\int\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{2}.

Both of these are finite constants depending only on
K
. Next,

\displaystyle\int\left[\int|f_{1}(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\frac{1}{h^{d}}\int\left|{K\left(\frac{x-y}{h}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\int\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\left[\int\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{2}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d}),\text{ and}

\displaystyle\int\left[\int|f_{2}(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\int\left|{\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\int K\left(\frac{x-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-z}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle\leq\int\left[\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\iint\left|{K\left(\frac{x-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-z}{h}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\iint\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(z^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\left[\int\left|{K\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\right]^{4}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d}).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\|{f_{1}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times\nu)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\iint\left[K\left(\frac{x-y}{h}\right)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\iint\left[K\left(x^{\prime}\right)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\|K\|_{L_{2}(\lambda)}^{2}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d}),

and

\displaystyle\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times\nu)}^{2}

\displaystyle=\iint\left[\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\int K\left(\frac{x-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-z}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{4d}}\iiiint K\left(\frac{x-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-z}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{x-w}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{y-w}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z\,\mathrm{d}w\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\iiiint K\left(z^{\prime}\right)K\left(z^{\prime}+\frac{y-x}{h}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}+\frac{y-x}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\iiiint K\left(z^{\prime}\right)K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}+x^{\prime}-z^{\prime}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}\nu(y)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d})\iiint K\left(z^{\prime}\right)K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}\right)K\left(w^{\prime}+x^{\prime}-z^{\prime}\right)\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{d}}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d})\iint\left|{K\left(z^{\prime}\right)K\left(x^{\prime}\right)}\right|\left[\int\left\{K\left(w^{\prime}\right)\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\right]^{1/2}\left[\int\left\{K\left(w^{\prime}+x^{\prime}-z^{\prime}\right)\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\right]^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d})\left[\int\left|{K\left(z^{\prime}\right)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}z^{\prime}\right]^{2}\left[\int\left\{K\left(w^{\prime}\right)\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}w^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{d}}\nu(\mathbb{R}^{d})\left\|{K}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda)}^{4},

which yields the result.

\hfill\blacksquare

Corollary F.2
.

If
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and kernel
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
,
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded density, and
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
satisfies
f(x,y)=f(y,x)
,
f(x,x)=\tau_{f}\in\mathbb{R}
and
f(x+c,y+c)=f(x,y)\in\mathbb{R}
for all
x,y,c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, then

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}\left[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\right]-n^{-1}\tau_{G[f]}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-3}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\qquad+n^{-2}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

for a constant depending on
K
and
P_{0}
.

Proof of
Corollary

F.2
.

Recall that
G[f](x,y):=\int f(s,t)K_{h}(s,x)K_{h}(t,y)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t
. Under the assumptions on
f
, we have
G[f](x,y)=G[f](y,x)
and
G[f](x,x)=\tau_{G[f]}
for all
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Since
(\hat{p}_{n}-\eta_{0,h})(x)=\int K_{h}(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(s)
, we can write

\displaystyle\int f\,\mathrm{d}\left[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\right]

\displaystyle=\int G[f]\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})].

By
Lemma

E.2
, we then have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}\left[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\right]-n^{-1}\tau_{G[f]}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{0}\left\{\int G[f]\,\mathrm{d}\left[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\right]-n^{-1}\tau_{G[f]}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-3}\int\left[\int G[f](x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\qquad+n^{-2}\left\|{G[f]}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}^{2}.

The result follows by
Lemma

F.1
.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.3
.

If
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded and
m
-times continuously differentiable density
\eta_{0}
with
\int[(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x<\infty
for all
\alpha
such that
|\alpha|=m
,
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and kernel
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
, and
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
satisfies
f(x,y)=f(y,x)
and
f(x,x)=\tau_{f}
for all
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, then

E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}\left[(P_{0,h}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\right]\right\}^{2}\lesssim n^{-1}h^{2m}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0,h})}^{2}.

Proof of
Lemma

F.3
.

We denote
f^{\circ}(y):=\int f(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0,h}-P_{0})(x)
. We then have

\displaystyle\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(P_{0,h}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]=(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})f^{\circ}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right].

Since
X_{1}^{*},\dotsc,X_{n}^{*}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathclap{\mathrm{iid}}}}{{\sim}}\hat{P}_{n}
,
E_{W}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]\left[f^{\circ}(X_{j}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]=0
for any
i\neq j
, so

\displaystyle E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j=1}^{n}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]\left[f^{\circ}(X_{j}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}E_{W}\left[f^{\circ}(X_{i}^{*})-\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left[\hat{P}_{n}(f^{\circ})^{2}-(\hat{P}_{n}f^{\circ})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\hat{P}_{n}(f^{\circ})^{2}.

By
Lemma

E.6
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left[n^{-1}\hat{P}_{n}(f^{\circ})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=n^{-1}P_{0,h}(f^{\circ})^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\int\left[\int f(x,y)[\eta_{0,h}(x)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\left|{\eta_{0,h}(x)-\eta_{0}(x)}\right|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}\iint|f(x,y)|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)\int[\eta_{0,h}(x)-\eta_{0}(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-1}h^{2m}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0,h})}^{2}.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.4
.

If
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and uniformly bounded kernel
K
,
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded density, then for any fixed
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}\lesssim n^{-1}\left[(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y),

where the constant in the bound depends on
P_{0}
and
K
.

Proof of
Lemma

F.4
.

We note that

\displaystyle E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{W}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(z)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\mathrm{Var}_{W}\left(\int f(x,Z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}E_{W}\left[\int f(x,Z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(z).

We bound the two terms in the final expression separately. We can write

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\iiint f(x,z)f(y,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\iiint f(x,z)f(y,z)(\hat{p}_{n}-\eta_{0,h})(x)(\hat{p}_{n}-\eta_{0,h})(y)\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\iiint|f(x,z)f(y,z)|\left\{E_{0}\left[\hat{p}_{n}(x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\left\{E_{0}\left[\hat{p}_{n}(y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]^{2}\right\}^{1/2}\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

Since
\eta_{0}
is uniformly bounded and
\int K^{2}<\infty
,

\displaystyle E_{0}\left[\hat{p}_{n}(x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{0}\left[\int K_{h}(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(s)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\mathrm{Var}_{0}\left[K_{h}(X,x)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{nh^{2d}}E_{0}\left[\left\{K\left(\frac{X-x}{h}\right)\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2d}}\int\left\{K\left(\frac{t-x}{h}\right)\right\}^{2}\eta_{0}(t)\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\int\left\{K\left(u\right)\right\}^{2}\eta_{0}(x+hu)\,\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{nh^{d}}

for each
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Thus,

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\iiint|f(x,z)f(y,z)|\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{d}}\int\left[\int|f(x,z)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z).

Next, we note that for each
x,y,z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, since
X_{1},\dotsc,X_{n}
are IID,

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\left[\hat{p}_{n}(x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[\hat{p}_{n}(y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[\hat{p}_{n}(z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left[\int K_{h}(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(s)\int K_{h}(t,y)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(t)\int K_{h}(u,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(u)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i,j,k}\left[K_{h}(X_{i},x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[K_{h}(X_{j},y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[K_{h}(X_{k},z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[K_{h}(X_{i},x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[K_{h}(X_{i},y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[K_{h}(X_{i},z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left\{\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[K_{h}(X,y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[K_{h}(X,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}.

Hence,

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\iiint f(x,z)f(y,z)\left[\hat{p}_{n}(x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[\hat{p}_{n}(y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[\hat{p}_{n}(z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}z\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\iiint f(x,z)f(y,z)\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\left[K_{h}(X,y)-\eta_{0,h}(y)\right]\left[K_{h}(X,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\int\left\{\int f(x,z)\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\left[K_{h}(X,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\,\mathrm{d}z.

Since
K
is uniformly bounded, we have
|K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0,h}(x)|\lesssim h^{-d}
for any
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|{\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\int\left\{\int f(x,z)\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\left[K_{h}(X,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\left|{\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\int\left\{\int f(x,z)\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}K_{h}(X,z)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\left|{\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\int\left\{\int f(x,z)\left[K_{h}(X,x)-\eta_{0,h}(x)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\eta_{0,h}(z)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{2d}}\left|{E_{0}\int\left\{\int|f(x,z)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}K_{h}(X,z)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|+\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{2d}}\left|{\int\left\{\int|f(x,z)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\eta_{0,h}(z)\,\mathrm{d}z}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{2}{n^{2}h^{2d}}\int\left\{\int|f(x,z)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z).

Putting the pieces together completes the result.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.5
.

If
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and uniformly bounded kernel
K
,
P_{0}
possesses a Lebesgue density function, and
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
satisfies
f(x,y)=f(y,x)
for all
x,y\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
then

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0,h})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{3}h^{d}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2},

where the constant in the bound depends on
K
only.

Proof of
Lemma

F.5
.

As in the proof of
Lemma

F.4
, we have

\displaystyle E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(z).

We first bound the first term. We have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right\}

\displaystyle=\int E_{0}\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\int\mathrm{Var}_{0}(f(X,z))\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\int E_{0}\left[f(X,z)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0,h})}^{2}.

Next, we note that

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(z)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}\left\{\int\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\int f(y,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(y)\int K_{h}(w,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(w)\,\mathrm{d}z\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\int E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i,j,k}\left[f(X_{i},z)-P_{0}f(\cdot,z)\right]\left[f(X_{j},z)-P_{0}f(\cdot,z)\right]\left[K_{h}(X_{k},z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad=\int E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[f(X_{i},z)-P_{0}f(\cdot,z)\right]^{2}\left[K_{h}(X_{i},z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\int E_{0}\left\{\left[f(X,z)-P_{0}f(\cdot,z)\right]^{2}\left[K_{h}(X,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right]\right\}\,\mathrm{d}z.

Since
K
is uniformly bounded, we have
\left|K_{h}(x,z)-\eta_{0,h}(z)\right|\lesssim h^{-d}
for all
x,z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\left|E_{0}\left\{\int\left[\int f(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})(z)\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{d}}\int E_{0}\left[f(X,z)-P_{0}f(\cdot,z)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{d}}\int E_{0}\left[f(X,z)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}z

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{d}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}.

Putting the two bounds together yields the result.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.6
.

If
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and uniformly bounded kernel
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
,
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded density, and
f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
satisfies
f(x,y)=f(y,x)
,
f(x,x)=\tau_{f}\in\mathbb{R}
and
f(x+c,y+c)=f(x,y)
for all
x,y,c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, then

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-n^{-1}\tau_{f}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{4}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f^{2}]}}{n^{3}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\left[1+(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)

Proof of
Lemma

F.6
.

We define
V_{n}(x,y):=\int f\,\mathrm{d}\left[(\delta_{x}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\delta_{y}-\hat{P}_{n})\right]
. We then have

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{i}^{*})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{i}^{*})-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}\right\}^{2}+2E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\right\}^{2}.

(31)

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
31
), by definition of
\tau_{f}
, symmetry of
f
, and adding and subtracting terms,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{i}^{*})-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\delta_{X_{i}^{*}}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\delta_{X_{i}^{*}}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}

\displaystyle\qquad=-\frac{2}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*})+\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})

\displaystyle\qquad=-\frac{2}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[\hat{P}_{n}\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})

\displaystyle\qquad=-\frac{2}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\frac{2}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[P_{0,h}\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n}).

Hence,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}+E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[P_{0,h}\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

(32)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})\right\}^{2}.

By
Lemma

F.4
, the first term on the right-hand side of (
32
) is bounded up to a constant by

n^{-3}\left[(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y).

For the second term on the right-hand side of (
32
),

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}[P_{0,h}\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}}E_{0}\left[\mathrm{Var}_{W}\left(\int f(x,X_{1}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(x)\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n^{3}}E_{0}\left[\int\left(\int f(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(x)\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left(\int f(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(x)\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y),

where the last inequality is because
K
is uniformly bounded. For the last term on the right-hand side of (
32
), we have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{3}}\sum_{i,j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}G[f](X_{i},X_{i})+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left\{\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\{\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{2}}+\frac{2\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}\sum_{i\neq j}E_{0}\left[G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right]+\frac{1}{n^{4}}E_{0}\left[\sum_{i\neq j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{4}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}E_{0}\left[G[f](X_{1},X_{2})\right]+\frac{1}{n^{6}}E_{0}\left[\sum_{i\neq j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right]^{2}.

By
Lemma

F.1
, we have
\left|E_{0}\left[G[f](X_{1},X_{2})\right]\right|\lesssim\|f\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}
. By
Lemma

E.1
and
Lemma

F.1
, we note that

\displaystyle E_{0}\left[\sum_{i\neq j}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{0}\left[4\sum_{i\neq j,j\neq k,k\neq i}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})G[f](X_{i},X_{k})+2\sum_{i\neq j}\left[G[f](X_{i},X_{j})\right]^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j,i\neq k,i\neq l\\
j\neq k,j\neq l,k\neq l\end{subarray}}G[f](X_{i},X_{j})G[f](X_{k},X_{l})\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{3}E_{0}\left[G[f](X_{1},X_{2})G[f](X_{1},X_{3})\right]+n^{2}E_{0}\left[G[f](X_{1},X_{2})\right]^{2}+n^{4}[E_{0}G[f](X_{1},X_{2})]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{3}\int\left(\int G[f](x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)+n^{2}\left\|{G[f]}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}^{2}+n^{4}\left\|{G[f]}\right\|_{L_{1}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{3}\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)+n^{2}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+n^{4}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}.

Combining these calculations, we get

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{4}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{4}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y).

Therefore,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})-\frac{\tau_{f}}{n}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{4}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{4}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\left[1+(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y).

For the second term on the right-hand side of (
31
), we note that for any
i\neq j
,

\displaystyle E_{W}\left[V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\mid X_{j}^{*}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{W}\left[f(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})-\int f(X_{i}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)-\int f(x,X_{j}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)+(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})f\mid X_{j}^{*}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\int f(x,X_{j}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)-(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})f-\int f(x,X_{j}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)+(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})f=0

Hence, by the law of total expectation,
E_{W}[V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})]=0
for all
i\neq j
, and by symmetry of
f
, which implies symmetry of
V_{n}
,
E_{W}[V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{k}^{*})]=0
for all
i\neq j\neq k
. Thus, by
Lemma

E.1
,

\displaystyle E_{W}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{W}\left[\frac{4}{n^{4}}\sum_{i\neq j,j\neq k,k\neq i}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{k}^{*})+\frac{2}{n^{4}}\sum_{i\neq j}\left\{V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\right\}^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+\frac{1}{n^{4}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\neq j,i\neq k,i\neq l\\
j\neq k,j\neq l,k\neq l\end{subarray}}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})V_{n}(X_{k}^{*},X_{l}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=E_{W}\left[\frac{2}{n^{4}}\sum_{i\neq j}\left\{V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{W}\left[V_{n}(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{W}\left[f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})-\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)-\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)+(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})f\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathrm{Var}_{W}\left[f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})-\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)-\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{W}\left[f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})-\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)-\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{W}\left\{f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})^{2}+\left[\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)\right]^{2}+\left[\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right]^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-2f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})\left[\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)\right]-2f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})\left[\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.+2\left[\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)\right]\left[\int f(x,X_{2}^{*})\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{W}\left\{f(X_{1}^{*},X_{2}^{*})^{2}-2\left[\int f(X_{1}^{*},y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)\right]^{2}+2\left[\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n^{2}}\int f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n}),

where the last inequality is because
[\int f\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})]^{2}\leq\int[\int f\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}
by Jensen’s inequality. Hence, by the assumption that
f(x+c,y+c)=f(x,y)
,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}V_{n}(X_{i}^{*},X_{j}^{*})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left[\int f^{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{2d}}\sum_{i,j}\iint\left\{f(s,t)\right\}^{2}K\left(\frac{s-X_{i}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t-X_{j}}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}\iint f(X_{i}+hs^{\prime},X_{j}+ht^{\prime})^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\iint\left\{f(X_{i}+hs^{\prime},X_{i}+ht^{\prime})\right\}^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{2}}E_{0}\left[\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}\iint\left\{f(X_{i}+hs^{\prime},X_{j}+ht^{\prime})\right\}^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}}\iint\{f(hs^{\prime},ht^{\prime})\}^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{n-1}{n^{3}}\iint E_{0}\left[f(X_{i}+hs^{\prime}-ht^{\prime},X_{j})^{2}\right]K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{3}}\iint\{f(hs^{\prime},ht^{\prime})\}^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}}\tau_{G[f^{2}]}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2},

where the last equality is because
f(x+c,y+c)=f(x,y)
for all
x,y,c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and the definition of
\tau_{G[f]}
. The result follows.

\hfill\blacksquare

Corollary F.7
.

If
\hat{P}_{n}
is the distribution corresponding to a kernel density estimator with bandwidth
h
and uniformly bounded kernel
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
,
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded density, and
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
, then for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
, we have
\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
,
\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]=n^{-1}\tau_{f}+o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
, and
\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]=n^{-1}\tau_{f}+o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

Proof of
Corollary

F.7
.

We note that
\int\left\{\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)=O(1)
for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
by
Lemma

F.1
since
P_{0,h}(\mathbb{R}^{d})=1
. Hence, by
Lemma

F.4
, we then have

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-1}\left[(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y)

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-1}\left[(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right],

which is
o(n^{-1})
if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
. This shows the first statement.

Next, we note that
\int\left\{\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)=O(1)
and
\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}=O(h^{-d/2})
for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
by
Lemma

F.1
. Hence, since
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded Lebesgue density function, by
Lemma

E.2
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]-n^{-1}\tau_{f}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-3}\int\left[\int f(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)+n^{-2}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-3}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)+n^{-2}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-3}+n^{-1}(nh^{d})^{-1},

which is
o(n^{-1})
if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
. This implies that
\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]=n^{-1}\tau_{f}+o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
.

Finally, since for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
, we have
f(x,y)=f(y,x)
,
f(x,x)=\tau_{f}
and
f(x+c,y+c)=f(x,y)
for all
x,y,c\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, under the stated conditions, by
Lemma

F.6
,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-n^{-1}\tau_{f}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{\tau_{G[f]}^{2}}{n^{4}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f^{2}]}}{n^{3}}+\frac{\tau_{G[f]}}{n^{3}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}+\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{3}}\left[1+(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left(\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y).

By
Lemma

F.1
, we have
\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{1}(\lambda\times P_{0})}=O(1)
,
\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}=O(h^{-d/2})
, and
\int\left\{\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)=O(1)
for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
. We also note that
\tau_{G[f]}:=\iint f(hu,hv)K(u)K(v)\,\mathrm{d}u\,\mathrm{d}v\lesssim h^{-d}
and
\tau_{G[f^{2}]}:=\iint\{f(hu,hv)\}^{2}K(u)K(v)\,\mathrm{d}u\,\mathrm{d}v\lesssim h^{-2d}
for each
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
, where the constants only depend on
K
. Thus,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-n^{-1}\tau_{f}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{h^{-2d}}{n^{4}}+\frac{h^{-2d}}{n^{3}}+\frac{h^{-d}}{n^{3}}+\frac{h^{-d}}{n^{2}}+\frac{1}{n^{3}}+\frac{h^{-2d}}{n^{5}}

\displaystyle=n^{-2}(nh^{d})^{-2}+n^{-1}(nh^{d})^{-2}+n^{-2}(nh^{d})^{-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+n^{-1}(nh^{d})^{-1}+n^{-3}+n^{-3}(nh^{d})^{-2},

which is again
o(n^{-1})
if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.8
.

If
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded and continuously differentiable density function
\eta_{0}
with
\int[(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x<\infty
for all
\alpha
such that
|\alpha|=1
,
\hat{P}_{n}
is a kernel density estimator with uniformly bounded kernel function
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
and bandwidth
h
satisfying
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
and
h\longrightarrow 0
,
\eta_{n}
is the density corresponding to
\hat{P}_{n}
, and
\eta_{n}^{*}
is a kernel density estimator based on the bootstrap data with the same kernel and bandwidth, then
(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n})-(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0})=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

Proof of
Lemma

F.8
.

As above, we define
\eta_{0,h}(x):=\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)
and
\eta_{n,h}(x):=\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)
. By adding and subtracting terms, we then have

\displaystyle(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{0})-(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad=(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n,h})+(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{0,h})+(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0,h})-(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0})

\displaystyle\qquad=\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]+\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\iint[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)]-\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\iint[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)]

By
Corollary

F.7
, if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
, then

\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})

and

\displaystyle\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\int K_{h}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left[n^{-1}\tau_{f_{1}}+o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})\right]-\left[n^{-1}\tau_{f_{1}}+o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}).

Finally, we write

\displaystyle\iint[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)]

\displaystyle=(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})g_{h},\text{ and}

\displaystyle\iint[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)]

\displaystyle=(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})g_{h}

for
g_{h}(x):=\int[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)
. As in the proofs of
Lemma

F.3
and
Lemma

F.6
, we have
E_{0}\left[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})g_{h}\right]^{2}\leq n^{-1}\|g_{h}\|_{L_{2}(P_{0})}^{2}
. By
Lemma

E.6
,
\int g_{h}(x)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x=O(h^{2})
, so
\|g_{h}\|_{L_{2}(P_{0})}^{2}=o(1)
as long as
h\longrightarrow 0
since
P_{0}
has uniformly bounded density. This implies that
\iint[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)=o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})g_{h}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{-1}E_{0}\|g_{h}\|_{L_{2}(\hat{P}_{n})}^{2}

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\int\left[g_{h}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(x)

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\iint\left[g_{h}(x)\right]^{2}K_{h}(x,y)\eta_{0}(y)\,\mathrm{d}y\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=n^{-1}\iint\left[g_{h}(x)\right]^{2}K(u)\eta_{0}(x+hu)\,\mathrm{d}u\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\lesssim n^{-1}\int\left[g_{h}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x.

By
Lemma

E.6
,
\int g_{h}(x)^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x=O(h^{2})=o(1)
. Thus,
\int[K_{h}(x,y)-\eta_{0}(x)]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.9
.

If
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded and
m
-times continuously differentiable density
\eta_{0}
with
\int[(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x<\infty
for all
\alpha
such that
|\alpha|=m
,
\hat{P}_{n}
is a kernel density estimator with uniformly bounded
m
th order kernel function
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
and bandwidth
h
satisfying
nh^{2d}\longrightarrow\infty
and
nh^{4m}\longrightarrow 0
,
\eta_{n}
is the density corresponding to
\hat{P}_{n}
, and
\eta_{n}^{*}
is a kernel density estimator based on the bootstrap data with the same kernel and bandwidth, then
\int[\eta_{n}(x)-\eta_{0}(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x-\int[\eta_{n}^{*}(x)-\eta_{n}(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

Proof of
Lemma

F.9
.

Recall that
f_{1}(x,y):=K_{h}\left(x,y\right)
and
f_{2}(x,y):=\int K_{h}(x,z)K_{h}(y,z)\,\mathrm{d}z
. As above, we denote
\eta_{0,h}(x):=\int K_{h}\left(x,y\right)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)
and
\eta_{n,h}(x)=\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(y)
. We then note that

\displaystyle\int\eta_{n}^{2}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}\times\mathbb{P}_{n}),\ \int(\eta_{n}^{*})^{2}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\times\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}),\ \int\eta_{0,h}^{2}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})

\displaystyle\int\eta_{n}^{*}\eta_{n}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\times\mathbb{P}_{n}),\ \int\eta_{n}^{*}\eta_{0,h}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\times P_{0}),\ \int\eta_{n}\eta_{0,h}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}\times P_{0})

\displaystyle\int\eta_{n,h}^{2}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\hat{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n}),\ \int\eta_{n}^{*}\eta_{n,h}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\times\hat{P}_{n}),\ \int\eta_{n}\eta_{n,h}=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}\times\hat{P}_{n}).

Hence, by adding and subtracting term, we have

\displaystyle\int(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n})^{2}-\int(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\int(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n,h}+\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n})^{2}-\int(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0,h}+\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=\int\left(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n,h}\right)^{2}+\int\left(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n}\right)^{2}+2\int\left(\eta_{n}^{*}-\eta_{n,h}\right)\left(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\int\left(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0,h}\right)^{2}-\int\left(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0}\right)^{2}-2\int\left(\eta_{n}-\eta_{0,h}\right)\left(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\{\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int\left(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n}\right)^{2}-\int\left(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-\mathbb{P}_{n})]-2\int(f_{2}-f_{1})\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times P_{0}].

By
Corollary

F.7
, we have

\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}).

By
Lemma

E.6
,
\int\left(\eta_{0,h}-\eta_{0}\right)^{2}=O(h^{2m})
, which is
o(n^{-1/2})
since
nh^{4m}\longrightarrow 0
. Next, we note that

\displaystyle\eta_{n,h}(x)-\eta_{n}(x)

\displaystyle=\int K_{h}(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(z)-\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)

\displaystyle=\int K_{h}(x,z)\eta_{n}(z)\,\mathrm{d}z-\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[\int K_{h}(x,z)K_{h}(y,z)\,\mathrm{d}z\right]\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)-\int K_{h}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)

\displaystyle=\int\left[f_{2}(x,y)-f_{1}(x,y)\right]\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)

\displaystyle=\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y),

where
f_{3}=f_{2}-f_{1}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle E_{0}\int(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n})^{2}

\displaystyle=E_{0}\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}\mathbb{P}_{n}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=E_{0}\int\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}f_{3}(x,X_{i})f_{3}(x,X_{j})\right\}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=E_{0}\int\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f_{3}(x,X_{i})f_{3}(x,X_{i})\right\}\,\mathrm{d}x+E_{0}\int\left\{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i\neq j}f_{3}(x,X_{i})f_{3}(x,X_{j})\right\}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\int E_{0}\left[f_{3}^{2}(x,X)\right]\,\mathrm{d}x+\frac{n-1}{n}\int\left\{E_{0}\left[f_{3}(x,X)\right]\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\left\|{f_{3}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}+\frac{n-1}{n}\int\left\{E_{0}\left[f_{3}(x,X)\right]\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x.

By
Lemma

F.1
, we have
\left\|{f_{3}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}\leq\left\|{f_{1}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}+\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}=O(h^{-d/2})
. By
Lemma

E.6
, we have

\int\left\{E_{0}\left[f_{3}(x,X)\right]\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\leq 2\int\left\{E_{0}\left[f_{1}(x,X)\right]-\eta_{0}(x)\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int\left\{E_{0}\left[f_{2}(x,X)\right]-\eta_{0}(x)\right\}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x=O(h^{2m}).

Hence,
\int(\eta_{n,h}-\eta_{n})^{2}=O_{P_{0}^{*}}(\{nh^{d}\}^{-1}+h^{2m})
, which is
o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
under the conditions
nh^{4m}\longrightarrow 0
and
nh^{2d}\longrightarrow\infty
.

Next, we have

\displaystyle\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-\mathbb{P}_{n})]

\displaystyle\qquad=\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]+\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(P_{0,h}-P_{0})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})].

By
Lemma

F.4
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}\lesssim n^{-1}\left[(nh^{d})^{-1}+(nh^{d})^{-2}\right]\int\left[\int|f_{2}(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(y),

which is
o(n^{-1})
under the condition
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
by
Lemma

F.1
. By
Lemma

F.3
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left[\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(P_{0,h}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right]^{2}\lesssim n^{-1}h^{2m}\|f_{2}\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0,h})}^{2},

which is
O(n^{-1}h^{2m-d})
by
Lemma

F.1
, which is
o(n^{-1})
by assumption. By
Lemma

F.1
and
Lemma

F.5
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0,h})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{3}h^{d}}\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}}\left\|{f_{2}}\right\|_{L_{2}(P_{0}\times P_{0,h})}^{2}+\frac{1}{n^{3}h^{2d}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{n^{2}h^{d}}+\frac{1}{n^{3}h^{2d}},

where the last inequality is because

\displaystyle\iint f_{2}^{2}(x,z)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\iint\left[\int K_{h}(x,s)K_{h}(z,s)\,\mathrm{d}s\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\iint\left[\iint K_{h}(x,s)K_{h}(z,s)K_{h}(x,t)K_{h}(z,t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{4d}}\iiiint K\left(\frac{x-s}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{z-s}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{x-t}{h}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times K\left(\frac{z-t}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{2d}}\left|\iiiint K\left(s^{\prime}\right)K\left(s^{\prime}+\frac{z-x}{h}\right)K\left(t^{\prime}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\times K\left(t^{\prime}+\frac{z-x}{h}\right)\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h^{d}}\left|\iiiint K\left(s^{\prime}\right)K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(t^{\prime}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\left.\times K\left(t^{\prime}+x^{\prime}-s^{\prime}\right)\eta_{0}(z-(x^{\prime}-s^{\prime})h)\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h^{d}}\iiiint\left|K\left(s^{\prime}\right)K\left(x^{\prime}\right)K\left(t^{\prime}\right)K\left(t^{\prime}+x^{\prime}-s^{\prime}\right)\right|\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}x^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(z)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{h^{d}}\left[\int K^{2}(x)\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}.

This implies that
E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]\right\}^{2}=o(n^{-1})
under the assumption
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
. Hence,
\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-\mathbb{P}_{n})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
if
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
.

Finally, by
Lemma

E.6
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left[\int(f_{2}-f_{1})\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times P_{0}]\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n}E_{0}\left[\int\left\{f_{2}(X,y)-f_{1}(X,y)\right\}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{2}{n}E_{0}\left[\int\left\{f_{1}(X,y)-\eta_{0}(X)\right\}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}+\frac{2}{n}E_{0}\left[\int\left\{f_{2}(X,y)-\eta_{0}(X)\right\}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=O(n^{-1}h^{2m}),

which is
o(n^{-1})
.

\hfill\blacksquare

Lemma F.10
.

If
P_{0}
possesses uniformly bounded and
m
-times continuously differentiable density
\eta_{0}
with
\int[(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(x)]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x<\infty
for all
\alpha
such that
|\alpha|=m
,
\hat{P}_{n}
is a kernel density estimator with uniformly bounded
m
th order kernel function
K
satisfying
\int K^{2}<\infty
and bandwidth
h
satisfying
nh^{2d}\longrightarrow\infty
and
nh^{4m}\longrightarrow 0
,
\eta_{n}
is the density corresponding to
\hat{P}_{n}
, and
\eta_{n}^{*}
is a kernel density estimator based on the bootstrap data with the same kernel and bandwidth, then
\mathbb{P}_{n}\phi_{n}-\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\phi_{n}^{*}=o_{P_{W}}(n^{-1/2})
.

Proof of
Lemma

F.10
.

Recall that
f_{1}(x,y):=K_{h}\left(x,y\right)
and
f_{2}(x,y):=\int K_{h}(x,z)K_{h}(y,z)\,\mathrm{d}z
. We define
f_{3}:=f_{1}-f_{2}
. We then have
\mathbb{P}_{n}\phi_{n}=2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}\times\mathbb{P}_{n})
and
\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\phi_{n}^{*}=2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\times\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}).
Hence, by adding and subtracting terms and the symmetry of
f_{3}
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}\phi_{n}^{*}-\mathbb{P}_{n}\phi_{n}\right)/2

\displaystyle\qquad=\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n}+\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h}+P_{0,h})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n}+\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h}+P_{0,h})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0}+P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0}+P_{0})]

\displaystyle\qquad=\left\{\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]+\left\{\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[P_{0,h}\times P_{0,h}]-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[P_{0}\times P_{0}]\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]+2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(P_{0,h}-P_{0})]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times P_{0}]+2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times P_{0,h}]-2\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times P_{0}].

We show each term on the right hand side of previous display is
o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
. First, we can use the same logic as in the proof of
Lemma

F.9
to show that

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})]-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times(P_{0,h}-P_{0})]=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}),\text{ and}

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})\times P_{0}]=o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}).

By
Lemma

F.1
and
Corollary

F.2
, we have

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int f\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]-n^{-1}\tau_{G[f]}\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim n^{-3}\int\left[\int|f(x,y)|\,\mathrm{d}x\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)+n^{-2}\left\|{f}\right\|_{L_{2}(\lambda\times P_{0})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=O(n^{-3})+O(n^{-2}h^{-d})

for
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
, which is
o(n^{-1})
because
nh^{d}\longrightarrow\infty
. Hence,

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]

\displaystyle\qquad=\int f_{1}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]-\int f_{2}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})]

\displaystyle\qquad=n^{-1}\left(\tau_{G[f_{1}]}+\tau_{G[f_{2}]}\right)+o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}).

Furthermore, we have
\tau_{G[f]}=O(h^{-d})
for
f\in\{f_{1},f_{2}\}
as shown above. Since
nh^{2d}\longrightarrow\infty
, we then have
n^{-1}(\tau_{G[f_{1}]}+\tau_{G[f_{2}]})=O(\{nh^{d}\}^{-1})=O(n^{-1/2}\{nh^{2d}\}^{-1/2})=o(n^{-1/2})
.

Next, we note that

\displaystyle\left|{\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0,h}\times P_{0,h})-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|{\iint\left[\iint f_{3}(s,t)K_{h}(x,s)K_{h}(y,t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|{\iint\left[\iint f_{3}(x+sh,y+th)K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|{\iiiint f_{3}(x,y+t^{\prime}h-s^{\prime}h)K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|{\iiiint f_{3}(x,y^{\prime})K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\eta_{0}(y^{\prime}+s^{\prime}h-t^{\prime}h)\,\mathrm{d}y^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})}\right|

\displaystyle\quad=\left|{\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]\left[\iint\left\{\eta_{0}(y+sh-th)-\eta_{0}(y)\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]\,\mathrm{d}y}\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left\{\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}y\right\}^{1/2}\left\{\int\left[\iint\left\{\eta_{0}(y+sh-th)-\eta_{0}(y)\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right\}^{1/2}.

We can write

\displaystyle\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\qquad\leq 2\int\left[\int f_{1}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)-\eta_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int\left[\int f_{2}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)-\eta_{0}(x)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x,

which is
O(h^{2m})
by
Lemma

E.6
. Since
\eta_{0}
is
m
-times continuously differentiable, for all
u
, a Taylor expansion with the Laplacian representation of the remainder gives

\displaystyle\iint\left\{\eta_{0}(y+sh-th)-\eta_{0}(y)\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad=\iint\left\{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq m-1}\frac{1}{\alpha!}(sh-th)^{\alpha}(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(y)\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\iint\left\{\sum_{|\alpha|=m}\frac{mh^{m}}{\alpha!}(s-t)^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{1}(1-r)^{m-1}(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(y+rh[s-t])\,\mathrm{d}r\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq m-1}\frac{1}{\alpha!}(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(y)\iint(sh-th)^{\alpha}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\qquad+\sum_{|\alpha|=m}\frac{mh^{m}}{\alpha!}\iint(s-t)^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{1}(1-r)^{m-1}(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(y+rh[s-t])\,\mathrm{d}rK(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t.

Since
K
is an
m
th order kernel function,
\iint(sh-th)^{\alpha}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t=0
for all
\alpha
such that
1\leq|\alpha|\leq m-1
. Defining
H(y,u,\alpha):=\int_{0}^{1}(1-r)^{m-1}(D^{\alpha}\eta_{0})(y+rhu)\,\mathrm{d}r
, we then have

\displaystyle\int\left[\iint\left\{\eta_{0}(y+sh-th)-\eta_{0}(y)\right\}K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\quad=\int\left[\sum_{|\alpha|=m}\frac{mh^{m}}{\alpha!}\iint(s-t)^{\alpha}H(y,s-t,\alpha)K(s)K(t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)

\displaystyle\quad=\int\sum_{|\alpha|,|\beta|=m}\frac{m^{2}h^{2m}}{\alpha!\beta!}\iiiint(s-t)^{\alpha}(s^{\prime}-t^{\prime})^{\beta}H(y,s-t,\alpha)H(y,s^{\prime}-t^{\prime},\beta)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times K(s)K(t)K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y).

The same method as in the proof of
Lemma

E.6
can be used to show that this is
O(h^{2m})
.
Hence,
\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0,h}\times P_{0,h})-\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}(P_{0}\times P_{0})=O(h^{2m})
, which is
o(n^{-1/2})
if
nh^{4m}\longrightarrow 0
.

We next consider the term
\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times P_{0}]
. As in the proofs of
Lemma

E.3
and
Lemma

E.4
,

\displaystyle E_{0}E_{W}\left\{\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})(x)\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq E_{0}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}\hat{P}_{n}(x)\right\}

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0,h}(x)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x,

where the last inequality is because
P_{0,h}
possesses uniformly bounded density. The last expression is
O(n^{-1}h^{2m})
by
Lemma

E.6
as shown above. Hence,
\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\mathbb{P}_{n}^{*}-\hat{P}_{n})\times P_{0}]=O_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}h^{m})=o_{P_{W}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

Finally, we consider the term
\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times P_{0,h}]
. We note that

\displaystyle\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times P_{0,h}]

\displaystyle=\iiiint f_{3}(s,t)K_{h}(x,s)K_{h}(y,t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\,\mathrm{d}(\mathbb{P}_{n}-P_{0})(x).

Hence,

\displaystyle E_{0}\left\{\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times P_{0,h}]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\iiint f_{3}(s,t)K_{h}(x,s)K_{h}(y,t)\,\mathrm{d}s\,\mathrm{d}t\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)

\displaystyle\qquad=\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\iiint f_{3}(x+s^{\prime}h,y+t^{\prime}h)K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{1}{n}\iiint\left[\int f_{3}(x+s^{\prime}h-t^{\prime}h,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}K(s^{\prime})K(t^{\prime})\,\mathrm{d}s^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}t^{\prime}\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(x)

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\int\left[\int f_{3}(x,y)\,\mathrm{d}P_{0}(y)\right]^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x.

As above, the last expression is
O(n^{-1}h^{2m})
, so that
\int f_{3}\,\mathrm{d}[(\hat{P}_{n}-P_{0,h})\times P_{0,h}]=O_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2}h^{m})=o_{P_{0}^{*}}(n^{-1/2})
.

\hfill\blacksquare
